2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Local PostgreSQL 18.4** (x86_64-windows, msvc-19.44.35227) on `::1/128:5432`, database `Final_Location_Analysis` (**46 GB**), connected as **`postgres` superuser**. Other databases: `postgis_36_sample` (29 MB, 0 conns), `postgres` (7857 kB, 0 conns). **No `sitesense` database exists.**
   - **`DATABASE_URL` == `ANALYTICS_DATABASE_URL`**. Two SQLAlchemy engines (`db/session.py:6`, `db/analytics_session.py:17`) point at the SAME database — the documented isolation does not exist.
   - **12 user schemas, 373 tables, 6 views, 0 materialized views.** inventory.txt covers 371; `topology`'s 2 tables are counted separately.
   - **Local: 0 RLS-enabled tables, 0 policies, 1 non-internal trigger** (`topology.layer :: layer_integrity_checks → layertrigger()`), **0 application triggers**, **47 FKs database-wide**, `pg_stat_database.stats_reset` NULL (counters cumulative).
   - **1796 local functions, only 9 non-extension, zero repo references to any of them:** `popfeat.levels_to_num(text)`; `public.fn_create_buffer(float8,float8,int4)`; `public.fn_get_complete_urban_analysis(float8,float8,int4)` and `(geometry)`; `public.fn_get_population_analysis(geometry)` and `(float8,float8,int4)`; `public.fn_income_analysis(text,text,date)`; `public.planet_osm_index_bucket(_int8)`; `public.planet_osm_member_ids(jsonb,char)`.
   - **6 views:** `popfeat.final`, `public.vw_latest_district_statistics`, plus 4 PostGIS catalog views. Neither user view is referenced by application code.
   - **Supabase project `lzdpjwmcjbhtkzkqudyt`, PostgREST 14.18, exactly 9 exposed tables:** `users`(11), `agent`(2), `company`(2), `role_change_audit`(11), `staff_invitations`(2), `property`(**0**), `property_photo`(**0**), `pano_jobs`(**0**), `user_tokens`(**0**). **4 exposed RPCs:** `is_admin`, `sitewise_is_active_staff`, `sitewise_is_active_admin`, `is_active_user` — none called from app code.

   - **Three property key-spaces:** TEXT catalogue (955 = 732 `MD-` + 223 `EP-`), BIGINT operational (1187 = 227 approved+published ids 89–315 + 955 pending ids 316–1270 + 5 drafts ids 1961–5216), analytics subjects (1849 = 894 P + 955 U).

   - **Disjoint-universe defect:** 227 published listings have **0** subject links and **0** photos (212 geospatial, 207 fnb_a); 955 pending imports have all 955 links + 5979 photos (270 geospatial). `detail_available = 0` for all 955 catalogue rows.
   - **Dual ownership:** `fyp.rental_properties.owner_id` bigint FK→`fyp.users(id)` (227 rows, all = legacy demo id 8) AND `owner_user_id` uuid (only 3 rows: f56fa3fa ×2, f69be94e ×1).

   - **Backend auth chain:** `bearer_token` → `SupabaseJWTVerifier` (local JWKS, ES256, leeway 30) or `SupabaseUserinfoVerifier` → `get_current_user` → `UserService.get_or_sync_current` → `get_active_user` → `get_staff_user` (`rbac.is_staff` + `aal == "aal2"`, else 403 `{"code":"staff_mfa_required"}`). Role/status always from the LOCAL row. **All 11 Supabase users have `factors=[]`.**
   - **Auth-audit root causes (unfixed, awaiting approval):** P0 `user_repository.py:97` hardcodes `role=entrepreneur, account_status=active`; P1 `user_service.py:73` never reconciles on login; P1 `security.py:176` reads a nonexistent `email_verified` JWT claim (`conftest.py:95` injects it, masking the bug); P1 `mobile_api_client.dart::_bearer()` has no refresh-and-retry.
   - **Frontends use Supabase Auth ONLY** — zero `.from()`/`.rpc()` in Dart; `auth_client.js` has 24 `client.auth.*` sites. All data via FastAPI :8090.
   - **Python env:** sqlalchemy 2.1.6, sqlmodel 0.0.48, **psycopg2 2.9.13**, supabase 2.34.0, httpx 0.28.1, alembic 1.20.1. **`psql`/`pg_dump` absent.**
   - **Sandbox:** Bash-spawned processes can only write inside the workspace; scratch dir = **`backend/.venv/.sw_db_audit`** (git-ignored via `backend/.gitignore:4:.venv/`). The Write tool CAN write to Temp but python cannot.
   - **psycopg2 gotcha:** pass `None` (not an empty tuple) as query vars, else literal `%` in `LIKE 'pg_temp_%'` is treated as a placeholder.
   - **Supabase MCP:** installed/authorized at app level (serverId `2cf015f2-f538-4320-8683-82be8d24e2e0`, 36 tools) but **NOT loaded in this session** (`mcp_list keyword=supabase` → 0). **No Supabase Postgres DSN configured anywhere.**
   - **PostgREST root OpenAPI trick:** `GET {SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/` with apikey+Bearer returns `definitions` and `paths` (`/rpc/*`). Exact counts via `Prefer: count=exact` + `Range: 0-0` → `content-range`.
   - **Two-pass reference scanning:** schema-qualified regex (strong evidence) then SQL-keyword/quoted-literal regex restricted to executable source (`.py/.sql/.dart/.js`), with manual exclusion of generic-word false positives.

     ```
     * ``fyp.rental_properties`` is the primary operational property table
       (bigserial ``id``). 1,182 rows live: 227 ``approved``+published,
       955 ``pending``+unpublished (the unlabeled research candidates).
     * ``owner_id`` → ``fyp.users.id`` (legacy bigserial identity). These ids are
       NOT Supabase ``auth.users``/``public.users`` uuids and must never be mixed.
     * ``fyp.property_subject_links`` maps a rental property to a
       ``sitewise_analytics.subject_features`` row (all 955 live links are
       ``match_verified``). The 955 linked subjects are class ``U``; the 894
       class ``P`` rows are PU training outlets with no rental_properties row.
     * ``sitewise_analytics.subject_features`` stores *features only* — there is
       no verified success-probability column. Never present one as such.
     * ``fyp.property_photos`` exists but is EMPTY live (the 5,979 image URLs of
       the brief were never imported). Reads must treat image absence as a normal
       state, not an error.          <-- STALE: it actually holds 5,979 rows
     * ``fyp.recommendation_results.user_id`` → ``fyp.users.id`` — personalised
       rows cannot be keyed to Supabase identities without a mapping that does
       not exist yet. The table is empty live.
     ```
